# ExitPass POS Server Annex E-1 Synthetic UAT Canonical Source Row and Hash Contract v1.2

## 1. Authority and artifact boundary

This document is normative for offline v1.2 artifact construction. It does not create data. Runtime-compatible families name the exact current canonicalizers; offline families cannot be persisted or treated as current runtime output. Controlled-clock, persisted deterministic identity, database loading, runtime, API, and migration changes remain blocked.

## 2. Scalar and collection encoding

All hashes use SHA-256 and lowercase hexadecimal. UTF-8 has no BOM. Offline strings are NFC. Compact offline JSON has no whitespace outside strings. Property order is the contract order, never dictionary iteration. The only escapes are `\"`, `\\`, `\b`, `\f`, `\n`, `\r`, `\t`, and lowercase `\u00xx` for remaining U+0000-U+001F; all other characters use literal UTF-8. Integers are unquoted base-10 with no plus or leading zero. Binary floating point and decimal fractions are prohibited. Boolean is `true` or `false`. Null is `null`; missing is absence and valid only where the family explicitly omits null; empty string is `""`; empty object is `{}`; empty array is `[]`.

Arrays use the family sort key and UUID text tie-breaker. Duplicate stable keys are invalid; byte-identical duplicate scalar array elements are invalid unless a family explicitly permits them (none in v1.2). Timestamps are UTC `yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffffZ`. Codes preserve documented case.

## 3. Hash families

| Family | Contract / runtime alignment | Normative preimage |
|---|---|---|
| Package content | `annex-e1-synthetic-package:sha256:v1.2`; offline only | LF records in exact order: profile, specification_id, case_id, row_count, zero-padded row entries, ej_count/entries, h_count/entries, annex_row_count/entries, outcome, evidence_count/entries; final LF required; values use offline JSON scalar rules; pair separator is literal `\|`, prohibited inside IDs/digests |
| Source object | `annex-e1-source-row:v1.2`; offline only | one compact JSON object ordered `contract,rowId,role,fields`; fields follow the row-family order; no BOM/LF |
| Fiscal-document request | runtime `sha256:v1` or `pos-server-fiscal-document-create:sha256:v2` | compact System.Text.Json object from `FiscalDocumentSemanticRequestHasher`; root keys sorted ordinal; nested field order fixed by its normalized SortedDictionary; null properties omitted by WhenWritingNull; arrays sorted by the runtime keys documented below |
| Workbook request | runtime `pos-server-annex-e1-request:sha256:v1` | compact Utf8JsonWriter object in exact AnnexE1SemanticHasher order; nullable correction fields present as null; sources ordered period sequence; fact arrays ordered fact ordinal then UUID |
| Accounting fact | runtime `pos-server-annex-e1-period-fact:sha256:v1` | compact Utf8JsonWriter object in exact AnnexE1SemanticHasher order; nullable fields always present |
| EJ semantic | runtime `pos-server-electronic-journal-event-semantic:sha256:v1` | exact 20 LF-delimited lines from `EJSEM`, including trailing LF |
| EJ integrity | runtime `pos-server-electronic-journal-integrity:sha256:v1` | exact 11 LF-delimited lines from `EJINT`, including trailing LF; previous hash precedes actor fields; genesis is 64 ASCII zeroes |
| Replay / conflict | current family hash comparison | replay reuses operation identity and requires byte-equal semantic preimage/hash; same identity plus unequal semantic hash is conflict and creates no authoritative row |


Fiscal-document arrays: discount details sort line sequence/null-last then type UUID; lines by line sequence; links by target UUID then link type; taxes by line sequence/null-last, tax type UUID, classification UUID; tenders by type UUID then normalized finality ref; totals by total type UUID. Reference-context objects sort keys ordinal. Null properties are omitted by that runtime family.

EJ facts are a compact JSON object whose keys sort ordinal; values are JSON string or null. EJ semantic preimage `EJSEM(row)` is exactly:

```text
profile=pos-server-electronic-journal-event-semantic:sha256:v1
event_schema=pos-server-electronic-journal-event:v1
site_pos_server_id=<site UUID>
fiscal_identity_id=<identity UUID>
currency=PHP
fiscal_reporting_period_id=<period UUID>
event_type=<event type>
source_transition_ref=<exact ledger reference>
source_transition_version=<exact ledger version>
effective_at=<UTC 7-digit timestamp>
fiscal_document_id=<UUID or empty>
fiscal_report_request_id=<UUID or empty>
x_z_report_id=<UUID or empty>
bir_sales_summary_report_id=<UUID or empty>
reprint_request_id=
fiscal_sequence_policy_id=<UUID or empty>
business_day_date=<yyyy-MM-dd>
idempotency_ref=<exact key>
facts=<exact ledger JSON>
<final LF>
```

`EJINT(row)` is exactly:

```text
profile=pos-server-electronic-journal-integrity:sha256:v1
event_reference=<EJ-streamN-sequence20 uppercase>
stream_sequence=<base-10>
recorded_at=<UTC 7-digit timestamp>
semantic_hash=<lowercase digest>
previous_integrity_hash=<lowercase predecessor or 64 zeroes>
actor_ref=<exact actor>
service_identity_ref=<exact service>
correlation_ref=<exact correlation>
retention_policy=fiscal_reconstruction_hold
<final LF>
```

Exact replay uses the identical normal preimage and digest. The semantic-conflict vector differs and must not mutate source or expected output. Reordered arrays have different digests. Null and omitted differ. Empty collection, Unicode, vertical-bar, and LF escaping are fixed by the vectors.

For every nullable field, the profile binding explicitly supplies null unless a listed runtime family omits null during hashing. Exact scenario/case values come from the mapping, monetary matrix, chronology, and identities; no row inherits an unstated value.

### 5.1 Closed universal value bindings

For scenario `NNN`, the Site code is `SYN-AE1-SPS-NNN`, fiscal identity code `SYN-AE1-FI-NNN`, serial `SYN-AE1-SERIAL-NNN`, MIN `SYN-AE1-MIN-NNN`, series `SI-NNN`, and fiscal number `SYN-AE1-SI-NNN-OOOO`. Actor is `synthetic-data-steward`, service is `pos-server-annex-e1-uat-preparer`, and case correlation is `DS-AE1-NNN`, except document commits whose actor, service, correlation, and idempotency are all the exact document-runtime values in the journal ledger. Currency is always `PHP`.

All contexts are the empty JSON object. All optional Central PMS, vendor, provider, customer, beneficiary, evidence, credential, link, reprint, external-delivery, signature, encryption, and correction-workbook fields are null. The only non-null Central PMS-shaped values are synthetic opaque references required by fiscal-document creation: `SYN-AE1-PARKING-NNN-OOOO`, `SYN-AE1-PAYMENT-NNN-OOOO`, `SYN-AE1-CONFIRM-NNN-OOOO`, and `SYN-AE1-FINALITY-NNN-OOOO`. They contain no personal or payment credential data.

Document operation key is `SYN-AE1-DOC-NNN-OOOO`; void operation key is `SYN-AE1-VOID-NNN-0004`; X, Z, and BIR keys are `SYN-AE1-X-NNN-PP`, `SYN-AE1-Z-NNN-PP`, and `SYN-AE1-BIR-NNN-PP`. Accounting fact key is `SYN-AE1-FACT-NNN-OOOO`; Annex key is `SYN-AE1-ANNEX-NNN-AA`; read/integrity/export attempts use `SYN-AE1-ACCESS-NNN-AA`. `PP` is two-digit period ordinal and `AA` is two-digit attempt ordinal.

Operation-request ordinals are closed in this order: all document creates, optional void, X/Z/BIR triples in period order, Accounting facts, Annex attempts, then access attempts. Request fields are operation key, exact scope IDs, business date or year/month, profile/version, requested-by actor, service, correlation, and the exact source object identities. No request contains a caller-supplied authority override.

Status-history transition 1 per document is null previous status to `recorded`; SP-A12 transition 5 is document 0004 `recorded` to `voided` with reason `SYNTHETIC_SAME_PERIOD_VOID`. X, Z, BIR, fact, and workbook requests transition from requested to committed only in expected-success rows. Replay creates no transition. Conflict and pre-commit recovery create no authoritative transition.

Each Accounting-fact audit binds its fact UUID, `created` action, `allowed` result, actor/service/correlation, exact semantic hash, and recorded time. Each committed workbook audit binds its workbook UUID and semantic/artifact hash references; artifact hash and length are evidence-capture fields and are not fixed before separately authorized generation. Each access audit binds the access operation, `read`, `export`, or `integrity_verify`, its `allowed` or `denied` result, scope, event time, and safe reason. Audit rows contain no raw request, raw response, secret, stack trace, personal identifier, or local path.

Source-object hashes use the exact row family field order above plus these bindings. Runtime request hashes use current runtime canonicalizers and their listed arrays. Journal hashes use section 8. Package hashes use ordered source-row hashes, then ordered journal hash pairs, headers H01-H10, rows D01-D32, outcomes, and evidence IDs. A missing binding is a validation failure; no default, environment value, current time, random value, or database-generated value may be substituted into an offline artifact.

## 6. Exact source profile bindings

A12 documents use (gross,discount,tax,net): SC (10000,2000,1200,9200), PWD (10000,2000,1200,9200), coupon (10000,1000,1200,10200), void (10000,0,1200,11200). B12 uses (10000,0,1200,11200). D12 uses (20000,0,2400,22400) and tenders cash 10000 plus digital wallet 12400.

Offline controlled-code UUIDs used inside exact journal fact strings are:

| Set/key | UUID |
|---|---|
| fiscal_tender_type/cash | `41fd35b9-07e4-5d54-a74a-c70119be6feb` |
| fiscal_tender_type/digital_wallet | `66a4e913-973b-5a42-8666-30b46b56db10` |
| fiscal_tax_classification/vatable | `bdd3f1e0-975a-579a-994a-723066e2b627` |
| fiscal_discount_privilege_type/statutory_peer | `ace40856-2b26-5145-9fea-3ed9d419b058` |
| fiscal_discount_privilege_type/coupon | `5b4810b7-a43e-5c71-a2c8-b00d85d59e86` |
| fiscal_total_type/final_payable | `2b476905-6209-59e2-ae0f-8f78b97e10b0` |

They are `OFFLINE_ARTIFACT_ONLY`; a future runtime comparison must substitute active resolved IDs and recompute document-event hashes.

## 7. Electronic Journal transition contract

There is one definition only. Document commit reference is `fiscal-document:<document UUID>`; version is `pos-server-fiscal-document-create:sha256:v2` for SC/PWD and `sha256:v1` otherwise. Void reference is `fiscal-document-void:<document UUID>:<idempotency key>`; version is `sha256:void:v1`. X/Z/BIR reference is `fiscal-report-request:<respective request UUID>`; version is `pos-server-fiscal-reporting:v1`.

Actor/service are `pos-server-fiscal-document-runtime` for commits; void actor is `synthetic-data-steward` and service `pos-server-fiscal-document-runtime`; report actor/service are `synthetic-data-steward` and `pos-server-annex-e1-uat-preparer`. Correlation and idempotency are the exact ledger values. Stream scope is Site POS Server + fiscal identity + PHP, not period.

## 8. Complete 148-row Electronic Journal ledger

Each table row binds `source-transition:q` and `electronic-journal-record:q` under its scenario. `source object` is the document, X/Z report, or BIR summary UUID. Causation identity is the same-ordinal source-transition UUID. Event schema is `pos-server-electronic-journal-event:v1`; stream version is `pos-server-electronic-journal-chronology:v1`; status is committed; retention is `fiscal_reconstruction_hold` starting at recorded_at with no deletion date; privacy is `SYNTHETIC_FISCAL_FACTS`; replay returns the same row; correction lineage is null. Semantic preimage is `EJSEM(row)`; integrity preimage is `EJINT(row)`. The byte count is canonical facts JSON only because no event artifact byte-length field is governed.

## 10. Offline/runtime separation

`INPUT_SOURCE` and `OFFLINE_ARTIFACT_ONLY` rows can be represented in a machine-readable offline package after a separate authorization. `EXPECTED_RUNTIME_RESULT` and `EXPECTED_REPORT_RESULT` are comparison records, not insert statements. Exact clock-generated values use `EXACT_AFTER_CLOCK_CONTROL`; deterministic persisted IDs are `STRUCTURAL_ONLY` until separately implemented. No current runtime capability is asserted for deterministic clocks, fixture IDs, C02 persistence, or offline catalog UUIDs.
